The little hook escaped three times before Cassian came to stand behind her.","\"An unusually hostile mechanism,\" he said.","\"It has been reading the papers.\"","He fastened it, then rested his hands at her waist. In the mirror she could see the fading mark at his temple from the storm and the bruise above her own wrist. They looked like people who had misplaced the peaceful part of being in love.","\"After this,\" she said, \"we are having a meal during which nobody delivers a document.\"","\"I'll have the dining room searched.\"","\"That sounds promisingly improper.\"","He kissed the place below her ear. She leaned back against him for a moment, closing her eyes. Then she took the silver flower from its case and put it around her neck for the last walk before the hearing.","The courthouse steps were crowded. June had arranged a place where the hospital residents who wished to attend could sit without being pushed into the media's path. Sister Agnes arrived with two umbrellas and the expression of a woman prepared to use either as a sword. Gideon said he had come for the architecture, which he considered a disappointment. Ada was carrying the accounts. Leda had stayed with her advocate, waiting for another call.","Mara saw her landlady in the second row of spectators. Mrs. Cress lifted the biscuit tin containing the receipt book as though it were a trophy.","Her courage settled. She was not walking into the room alone.","The first question from the opposing attorney concerned the family-history entry. He read out the invented title with the slow satisfaction of a man unveiling a stain. Then he asked whether Mara accepted its withdrawal.","\"Yes. I asked for the description to be corrected when it first appeared.\"","\"Yet you continued to enjoy its benefits.\"","\"Which benefit are you asking about?\"","\"Access to prominent people. Considerable publicity. Social standing.\"","\"I would gladly have returned the publicity. The people would have had to make their own arrangements.\"","Somebody behind her made a sound suspiciously like a laugh. The judge looked up. Mara straightened. She had promised counsel she would not turn the hearing into a duel merely because she could see an opening.","The attorney produced a publicity page bearing the copied signature. Mara's counsel supplied the original correction, its date, and the receipt book from which the signature appeared to have been taken. The judge directed that the contested approval page be examined with the other material. The private register's withdrawal did not alter the hospital survey authorization, which had been granted in Mara's own name.","The judge also asked whether any hospital grant made noble ancestry a condition of payment. The trust's letter contained no such clause. The opposing attorney suggested that public acclaim had nevertheless influenced the selection.","\"Then identify the actual decision and the person responsible,\" the judge said. \"This court will consider a claim about wrongdoing. It will not restore feudalism by treating family rank as a qualification for useful work.\"","Mara lowered her gaze before her expression could become unsuitable. Beside counsel's papers lay her little fellowship card, bent at one corner from months of use. She had earned that entry at a workbench. No publication had the authority to make the work disappear.","The question of the stolen object remained, hard and separate. She could enjoy this small reversal without mistaking it for the end of the suit.","The attorney tried once more to call her the former heiress.","\"Miss Vale will do,\" the judge said.","Mara kept her face still. Inside, something bright and savage applauded.","Then the flower was called for.","She unfastened the chain. The motion was familiar from a hundred repairs and suddenly difficult. Elena's folded petal lay warm against her fingers. Mara placed the mechanism in its padded case and watched the clerk carry it to the table.","\"Did you remove this object from the saleroom without authorization?\" the judge asked.","\"Yes.\"","\"Why?\"","\"My mother had worked on it. I believed her work and material inside it had been wrongfully taken. I wanted them back.\"","\"Had ownership of the entire object been established?\"","\"No.\"","The answer cost her. She had corrected her account once before, when the museum register showed that Elena had restored an older mechanism rather than made all of it. Saying it in this room felt like handing an opponent the narrowest part of a blade.","\"I should not have treated my belief as permission to take it,\" she continued. \"I will answer for the taking. I am asking that the records associated with it be protected while their history is examined.\"","The room was quiet now. There was no clever reply available, and she was relieved not to have to find one.","Her counsel explained the divided custody established months earlier: the magnetic strip, the mechanism, the invoice, and the documented copies had not all traveled together. An independent inventory would list them separately. Cassian supplied the handling record through counsel. He did not make a speech about her character.","The opposing attorney wanted the collection returned immediately to the auction representative. Mara felt her fingers close around the edge of the chair. If the components disappeared into the same chain of private transfers they were investigating, they might never assemble them again.","The judge ordered temporary neutral custody instead. The object and associated originals would be sealed, inventoried, and examined under arrangements agreed through the court. The ownership dispute and any further consequences of the unauthorized removal remained to be determined. Mara would provide an account of the physical handling and make a personal deposit towards verified preservation costs. Hospital funds were expressly excluded.","She had expected losing the flower from her neck to feel like losing her mother again. It did not. Elena was sitting in a room with a blue blanket, deciding whether she wanted visitors. The silver flower could be important without being the only place Mara kept her.","The clerk showed her the sealed case. She checked the description and signed the inventory.","Outside, the reporters began shouting before she reached the bottom step. Had the court confiscated the evidence? Had she been convicted? Was the duke paying her way out?","June moved beside her with the complete order on a tablet. Mara gave one short answer, identifying the temporary custody and the unresolved questions, then turned towards the car. She would not spend the rest of the day making fragments easy to steal from her mouth.","A boy near the rail held out a folded newspaper. \"My grandmother asked me to give you this.\"","Across the photograph of Mara beneath the false headline, someone had written: SHE FIXED MY CLOCK. THAT PART WAS TRUE.","Mara stared at the uneven letters. The boy seemed embarrassed by her silence.","\"Would you thank her for me?\" she asked.","He nodded and ran back through the crowd.","In the car, Mara checked the personal deposit against her remaining grant payment. The figures were uncomfortable but possible. Cassian glanced at the screen and put a small packet beside her.","\"If that is money, I may become difficult.\"","\"It is a biscuit. I have prepared for the possibility.\"","She opened it, laughing despite herself. There were two. He had remembered the broken ginger biscuit from the academy and somehow found the same kind.","\"You are dangerously observant,\" she said.","\"Only where it matters.\"","She broke one in half and gave him the larger piece. The court had taken the flower into custody, the papers had taken her morning, and strangers had tried to take her name. They could not have this small exchange. She leaned over, kissed the corner of his mouth, and tasted ginger.","At Mercy House, Elena was asleep. A pencil lay beside a half-finished drawing, its flower missing two petals. Mara sat with it until Noor came in. She wanted to tell her mother everything and spare her all of it. The two wishes pulled in opposite directions.","\"She asked whether you had lunch,\" Noor said.","\"I'm beginning to suspect a conspiracy.\"","\"An admirable one. Go and participate. I'll tell you when she wakes.\"","Cassian was waiting in the workshop with a tray. It held bread, a pot of soup, and no official envelope. Mara inspected every corner before sitting down.","\"I kept my word,\" he said.","She touched the empty place at her throat. \"It feels strange.\"","\"I know.\"","\"I thought I'd feel brave when I admitted it. I mostly wanted to throw the table at that man.\"","\"The table was a poor choice. Too many witnesses.\"","She laughed, and the last of the courtroom's grip loosened. He drew her chair closer with his foot and kissed her, warmly enough that she forgot what she had meant to say about the soup. When he drew back, she found herself holding his sleeve with both hands.","\"There you are,\" he murmured.","\"An expensive place to look.\"","\"Worth the journey.\"","A knock interrupted them. Mara put her forehead against his chest and groaned. Cassian looked towards the door with such concentrated displeasure that whoever stood outside wisely knocked again more softly.","It was Rowan, carrying no document. He appeared to understand at once why this mattered.","\"I bring news in the ancient oral tradition,\" he said. \"The communications invoice has a matching payment. The authorization came from a foundation campaign account. Someone budgeted for your invented ancestry before the first dinner.\"","Mara sat up.","\"And,\" Rowan added, \"the hospital's management contractor has called an extraordinary neighborhood meeting. They want a new local representative for the trust's review. Preferably someone less troublesome.\"","Mara looked at Cassian, then at the newspaper beside her bag.","\"I might know a candidate,\" she said."],"questions":[{"type":"purpose","stem":"Why is the At least, that was how his spokesman introduced him. Mara thought a statesman ought to carry something more convincing than a portable banner and three assistants with matching haircuts.","His name was Victor Saye. He had a law degree, an elegant suit, and a plan to make the district manageable again. The banner called this A NEW BASIS FOR CONFIDENCE. Someone had used a photograph of Mercy House taken before the roof was repaired.","\"They might have asked us for an up-to-date picture,\" Ada said.","\"Then it would be harder to advertise the rescue,\" Mara replied.","The meeting occupied the former despatch hall beside the canal. A Victorian statue watched over the entrance with priestly gloominess. Its polished bench had room for six people, although fourteen were trying to fit into the shade. Inside, the air smelled of floor cleaner and cherry cake. Gideon had brought the cake to improve electoral participation. Sister Agnes had brought a gallon of tea and a prohibition on smoking.","Mara had come to stand for the temporary neighborhood seat in the trust's review. She had not expected the candidate table to be placed on a platform. The spotlight made her empty throat feel conspicuous. She touched the collar where the flower used to rest, then put her hand down.","Cassian sat in the audience. For once nobody tried to supply him with a ceremonial chair. Admiral had accompanied him and was asleep solidly across his shoes. Mara considered this the dog's most useful political judgement so far.","Saye opened with the latest headlines. The district, he said, needed an ally who could count on government cooperation, rather than a high-profile controversialist whose mere presence might induce a lockout of funding. He spoke about ethics, equity, and the importance of legally consistent procedures. Every sentence made the same offer: choose someone easier to control, and uncertainty would disappear.","When he had finished, the moderator asked Mara for her response.","\"Which funding is liable to be locked out?\" she asked.","Saye smiled. \"A broad range of assistance.\"","\"The monthly patient-support payment? The recovery grant? The evening immunization clinic? They're administered through different accounts. Which one do you mean?\"","\"We must consider the wider feasibility of our arrangements.\"","Ada placed the accounts on the table. \"Then the attachment with the payment dates may be useful.\"","A woman in the second row stood. Her husband was receiving cancer care elsewhere and came to Mercy House for supported meals between appointments. She wanted to know whether he would still have somewhere to eat next Tuesday. Saye spoke about socialization, self-image, and restoring confidence among external contributors.","\"He doesn't require a new self-image,\" she said. \"He requires soup.\"","The room applauded. Mara kept her smile to herself. It was becoming her favorite campaign.","She explained the current position in plain terms. There were bills to pay and contracts to challenge. Some payments were secure, some depended on forthcoming decisions, and none would become plentiful because a well-dressed man called the district a model. She would not overstate what a neighborhood representative could do. She could insist that a refusal reach the people affected, with an accurate reason and a name attached.","Saye asked whether she intended to turn every administrative difficulty into a nuisance-lawsuit.","\"No,\" Mara said. \"I intend to turn some of them into lunch.\"","The laugh reached the back of the hall. She waited for it to ebb. \"We have already reopened a kitchen, repaired equipment, and found where money was going. I can build on that work. I can't promise utopia. If someone does, ask whether the upkeep is incorporated in the price.\"","A man who described himself as a lifelong democrat rose to ask about her bank account. Had the duke paid her costs at court? Was her campaign an extension of his influence?","Mara gave the figure she had deposited from her own earnings. She did not give Cassian's financial details. Then she looked towards the back, where he was attempting to move his foot without waking Admiral.","\"He is here because I asked him to come. His professional assistance has been declared wherever it matters. His vote, if he had one in this district, would still count as one.\"","\"Do you advise him how to vote?\" somebody called.","Cassian looked up. \"She rarely stops at advice.\"","The room laughed again, and Mara felt herself blush. It was annoying how easily he could rouse that response from her while she was engaged in the serious business of being respectable.","Then the lights went out.","For two seconds, everyone remained still. A child began to cry near the entrance. The bright emergency sign above the door flickered and died. One of Saye's assistants tried the handle and found that the old powered latch had stuck.","Mara left the platform before the moderator could summon order. \"Nobody push. There's another exit by the kitchen. Agnes, can you take the people at the front that way?\"","The room adapted more quickly than the official plan. Ada opened her phone's light. Cassian lifted Admiral clear of the aisle and helped a man with a walking frame towards the side passage. Gideon kept up a running complaint about the cake, which gave the frightened child something ordinary to listen to.","The kitchen exit opened onto a yard, but a delivery trolley blocked its outside edge. Mara squeezed through, dragged the trolley clear, and pushed back the panel so the door would stay open. Cold air rushed in. The first residents came through without being hurried.","A contractor's overseer appeared beside the power cupboard. He said the supply had been shut down during a dispute over a temporary service payment. The building's account was not the hospital's account, but the same management company handled both. An automated instruction had been sent that morning.","\"Can you restore it?\" Mara asked.","\"I need authorization.\"","She pointed towards Saye, who had followed them into the yard. \"Your campaign has been emphasizing its excellent connection with the contractor. This would be a timely demonstration.\"","For the first time all afternoon, he had no polished paragraph ready. Then he reached for his phone. Mara let him make the call. The point was to get the door working, not deny a competitor the chance to do something good.","The supply returned fifteen minutes later. Nobody wanted to go back inside, so they brought the ballot box into the yard. Gideon cut the cake on a clean board. A reporter asked whether the outage had been a campaign stunt. June, standing beside him, said she would ascertain the instruction's history before suggesting who had arranged it.","The vote was counted twice. Mara won by a margin large enough that the moderator stopped trying to make the announcement sound neutral.","For a moment the yard seemed to tip around her. Then Sister Agnes pulled her into an embrace so brisk that Mara lost both her balance and the ability to give an elegant response. Ada was grinning. Cassian stood beyond them with Admiral's lead in one hand, watching her as if the afternoon had produced something priceless.","Saye offered a formal handshake. Mara accepted it. \"Thank you for making the call.\"","\"A necessary civil courtesy,\" he said stiffly.","\"One we can build on.\"","A porter waited until the applause ended before asking about unpaid evening shifts. The contractor called them voluntary assistance; the staff called them the hours in which patients still needed help. Mara wrote down both descriptions and asked for the rosters and monthly statements.","\"Labour-management relations,\" Saye observed, with the relief of finding a suitably large phrase.","\"People's wages,\" the porter replied.","Mara promised a place for the issue in the review. Adherence to a budget could not mean treating the smallest pay packet as negligible while a management fee remained unchangeable. It was likely to be an unpopular subject with the contractor. The thought no longer frightened her as much as it had that morning.","The residents gathered to assign actual tasks. A representative was expected to answer messages, attend the review, and hear complaints without describing their owners as a mob. Mara would have office hours, a notebook, and no extra salary. The celebration acquired a practical lining almost at once. She found the weight of it strangely comfortable.","Outside the gate, Cassian took the empty cake tin from her.","\"You were magnificent,\" he said.","\"I promised to be a useful nuisance.\"","\"An understatement.\"","She leaned into him and kissed him in full view of the hall, the banner, and whatever mass media remained. Somewhere behind them, Gideon gave a triumphant whistle. Cassian's free hand settled at her waist. For once, Mara did not care what story the photograph would become.","June approached only after they separated. Her expression changed the afternoon's balance again.","The supposedly automatic power instruction carried a priority code used by the emergency infrastructure office. The office had not yet confirmed who requested it. The same code appeared on the latest proposal for citywide network protection.","Someone was testing how easily an ordinary service could be turned off."],"questions":[{"type":"inference","stem":"Why Its advertisement asked whether a thief should be allowed to lead a community.","June placed the two versions side by side on the taxi seat. \"A remarkable expansion of the theme.\"","\"Would it be dishonest to say I'm looking forward to it?\" Mara asked.","\"Only slightly.\"","Cassian had offered to come inside. Mara asked him to wait until the interview ended. The studio wanted the duke in the audience and had already designed a shot in which his reaction would become part of every answer she gave. He had looked at the proposed seating plan, then asked which nearby shop sold the ginger biscuits she liked.","It was a superb interpretation of support.","In the makeup room, a headhunter mistook June for Mara's agent and offered future television work. June said the client was engaged in running a hospital project and had limited enthusiasm for becoming content. The man gave her a card anyway. Its gold printing was so thick that Mara thought it could serve as a small weapon.","The host, Gareth Lyle, came in smiling. He had once made entertaining travel programs about the countryside. His new show covered politics, morality, and other subjects on which guests could be persuaded to interrupt each other.","\"No hard feelings about the advertisement,\" he said. \"We need a selling point.\"","\"I thought that was the social mobility.\"","\"Our listeners tend to prefer people to sociology.\"","\"A fortunate preference for people.\"","His smile flickered. June hid hers behind the release form. She had added the agreed limits in red ink: no unauthorized patient footage, no disclosure of clinical details, and no implication that a pending judicial proceeding had reached a verdict. The producer signed them. Gareth was impatient to begin.","Mara had written a short statement for June's article that morning. While the cameras were adjusted, she read it once more on her phone.","\"A citizen does not acquire dignity through a designer surname. Neither does accepting public responsibility give anyone a stress-free claim to admiration. Questions about conduct belong in journalism; questions about ancestry become a distraction when the work was never contingent on ancestry. The objective should be to establish who made a decision, what resources were used, and whom the decision affected. If coverage substitutes prestige for records, it helps the well-connected whether it praises them or attacks a convenient outsider. The practical remedy is modest: retain the whole answer, identify the source, and give a correction room to catch up with an error.\"","She put the phone away. June gave her a small nod. The paragraph was formal; the interview would not be. Mara preferred to remember the principle and let the words arrive for themselves.","The opening question was the one from the advertisement. Did she believe an ambitious woman could simply take property when she felt morally entitled to it?","\"No,\" Mara said.","Gareth waited for a speech. She let him wait.","\"Yet you removed the flower.\"","\"Yes. I have acknowledged the action, and the court is dealing with it. My hospital work and the invented genealogy are separate questions. Which would you like to discuss?\"","He chose the genealogy. A large screen showed the false approval page. Mara asked for the old invoice image to be displayed beside it, with personal details covered as agreed. June had supplied it through counsel's published statement. The overlapping payment square was visible on both.","\"Your account is that somebody copied the signature,\" Gareth said.","\"That is the concern under examination. Your caption says I approved the genealogy. The original source needs to be checked before you pronounce either conclusion.\"","\"A convenient way to avoid a clear answer.\"","Mara looked at the screen, then back at him. \"I did not approve it. That answer is clear. The work of finding out who assembled the page takes longer than a caption.\"","The audience laughed. Gareth laughed with them a second too late.","He moved to money. Mercy House was nearly bankrupt; its assets were illiquid; the trust had inherited massive obligations. Was the hospital an unrealistic project sustained by a rich lover's generosity?","Mara described the kitchen contract, the verified repairs, and the records Rowan had helped release. She gave the scale of the remaining shortfall rather than pretending the first improvements had solved it. When Gareth called the figures modest, she asked whether he would consider three extra days of meals modest if they were the days he needed.","For the first time, he looked beyond the next question.","Then the screen changed.","Elena appeared in a hospital chair, filmed from outside a partly open door. Her hand was searching for a pencil. The picture had been enlarged until her uncertainty filled the wall. A caption asked what her daughter had to hide.","Mara stood.","The producer made a sharp movement behind the camera. Gareth began to say that the clip illustrated the human dimension. Mara walked to the screen and stood in front of the image. Her pulse was beating in her throat, but her voice was steady.","\"You signed an agreement about patient footage. Turn it off.\"","\"This is already in circulation—\"","\"That is not consent. She is a grown-up woman recovering from harm, not a texture for your discussion.\"","In the audience, a man lowered his phone. A woman beside him said, audibly, \"Turn it off.\"","The floor manager looked towards the producer. Someone in the control room hesitated. Mara could see the delay growing into a decision nobody wanted to own.","June rose from the side bench. \"The agreement is here. The whole request is being recorded.\"","The image disappeared. The replacement was a blue field so plain that it seemed almost miraculous. Gareth tried to announce a short break.","\"Before the break,\" Mara said, \"tell the audience where you got the clip.\"","\"That is confidential.\"","\"Then say you are protecting its supplier. Don't call it my mother's story.\"","The silence was louder than applause. The producer took off her headset and came onto the floor. She apologized to Mara and said the clip would be removed from the replay. An internal review would establish how it had entered a program whose conditions expressly excluded it.","Mara accepted the practical promise without making forgiveness part of the broadcast. She asked that the apology be retained in the recording, then sat down. Her knees were less reliable than she wanted the cameras to know.","Gareth had four remaining questions and no natural route to any of them. He asked what she wanted to do next.","\"Finish the meal delivery plan,\" Mara said. \"Attend the trust review. See whether my mother wants a visitor. I have enough actual responsibilities without the ones invented for television.\"","The audience applauded before the closing music began.","In the corridor, June's phone lit up with messages. Some praised Mara; some were predictably scornful. More important was a note from the producer asking counsel to preserve the footage request and release form. The supplier had used an account that appeared in the foundation's publicity records.","\"I won't publish the account name until we verify it,\" June said. \"But I think somebody has paid for a very expensive error.\"","Mara leaned against the wall. The anger had left her exhausted. She wanted a bathroom, a chair, and a world in which she did not have to defend Elena's right to look tired.","Cassian came through the door carrying a paper bag. He took one look at her face and handed it to June. Then he drew Mara into the quiet space beside the fire exit.","\"I saw,\" he said.","She pressed her forehead to his chest. His coat smelled of cold air and the bakery. \"I nearly threw the chair.\"","\"A persistent temptation.\"","\"It was a much lighter chair than the one at court.\"","\"You are learning.\"","She laughed shakily. He kissed her temple, then her mouth, and she let the noise of the studio fall away. When they separated, June was studying the biscuit bag with the disciplined concentration of a journalist protecting a source.","\"There are six,\" June said. \"I have counted twice.\"","Mara took one. \"We shall need a fair distribution.\"","\"A democratic principle I can support,\" Cassian said.","Noor called while they finished the biscuits. Elena knew there had been a broadcast but had chosen not to watch the replay. She wanted Mara to know that she did not need an apology from her daughter.","\"She does have a request,\" Noor said.","Mara straightened. \"What is it?\"","\"A pencil that doesn't break. And a visit during which nobody asks her to remember anything.\"","Mara closed her eyes. She had been ready for a disaster, an accusation, another demand to explain. The ordinary request was harder to answer without crying.","\"I'll bring three pencils,\" she said.","\"And yourself. The second item seems to be the chief objective.\"","Cassian took the empty bag from her hand. They would stop at a shop on the way.","Her phone rang. It was Amina Holt.
